<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量环（TR）分解天然适合表示周期系统，但闭环几何使其缺乏全局规范形式，导致环境成本高且病态。现有周期 DMRG 虽通过低秩压缩环境将局部运算降至 O(pχ³)，但两站点形式的截断步骤仍需考虑周围环境，数值稳定性与效率有待改进。

Method: 将低秩环境压缩方法扩展到高效的两站点 Ring-DMRG 算法；通过合适的规范变换与广义 Davidson 求解器增强数值鲁棒性；针对两站点截断：当环境可分时，用适当的框架变换将其化简为普通 SVD，当环境不可分时，采用保留完整环境的交替优化。

Result: 张量环相对张量列的计算优势取决于所需环境秩 p 随键维和系统尺寸的标度。在临界系统中，发散的相关长度使长程观测量在热力学极限下仍对周期几何敏感，但达到可观测量固定精度所需的环境秩不随系统尺寸增长。因此 Ring-DMRG 保持与标准 DMRG 相同的键维立方标度，却在显著更小的键维下实现。低秩环境构造还给出置信传播（BP）的系统性推广，标准 BP 在秩一极限下被恢复。

Conclusion: Ring-DMRG 在临界系统中以不随系统尺寸增长的环境秩实现了与标准 DMRG 相同的立方标度，但所需键维更小；低秩环境构造统一推广了 BP，标准 BP 为其秩一特例。

Abstract: Tensor-ring (TR) decompositions provide a natural representation of periodic systems but are difficult to optimize because the closed geometry prevents a global canonical form and leads to costly, ill conditioned environments. Existing periodic DMRG methods alleviate this difficulty by compressing long environments to a low-rank representation, reducing local operations to $\mathcal{O}(pχ^3)$, where $p$ is the retained environment rank. Here, we extend this approach to an efficient two-site Ring-DMRG algorithm and improve its numerical robustness through appropriate gauge transformations and a generalized Davidson solver. A central challenge in the two-site formulation is the truncation step, which must account for the surrounding environment. When this environment is sufficiently separable, a suitable change of frame reduces the truncation to an ordinary SVD. When it is not separable, we instead use an alternating optimization that retains the full environment.
  The computational advantage of tensor rings over tensor trains depends on the scaling of the required environment rank $p$ with bond dimension and system size. For critical systems, the divergent correlation length makes long-range observables remain sensitive to the periodic geometry as the thermodynamic limit is approached. In this regime, the environment rank required to reach a fixed accuracy in the observable does not grow with the system size. Ring-DMRG therefore retains the same cubic scaling with bond dimension as standard DMRG, but at a substantially smaller bond dimension. The low-rank environment construction also provides a systematic generalization of belief propagation (BP), with standard BP recovered in the rank-one limit.

</details>


### [85] [Fourth-order perturbation theory for the Frohlich polaron: Analytic structure of the weak-coupling series and the crossover to strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10030)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文计算了三维Fröhlich极化子基态能量与有效质量的Rayleigh-Schrödinger微扰级数（至耦合常数α的四阶），其中三阶与四阶有效质量系数为新结果。由于Fröhlich相互作用是固定总动量下孤立非简并基态的形状有界微扰，该级数具有非零收敛半径。系数呈现高度规律性，可用α≈8.5处指数约1.4的幂律奇异性描述，该奇异性对应向强耦合的渡越，弱耦合级数能准确定位但不能穿过它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fröhlich极化子问题长期以来依赖微扰展开与变分方法，但其弱耦合级数的收敛性质及向强耦合的过渡机制尚不清楚。作者希望借助形状有界微扰理论确立级数的非零收敛半径，并用适用于收敛级数的比值法与Padé方法分析系数规律，从而刻画弱、强耦合之间的渡越。

Method: 对三维Fröhlich极化子的基态能量和有效质量，将Rayleigh-Schrodinger微扰级数计算到耦合常数α的四阶，其中有效质量的三阶与四阶系数为新推导结果。利用Fröhlich相互作用作为固定总动量下孤立非简并基态的形状有界微扰这一性质，论证级数具有非零收敛半径。随后采用比值法和Padé近似处理该收敛级数，并用弱耦合系数构造Padé近似。

Result: 系数表现出显著的规律性，可由α≈8.5附近、指数约1.4的简单幂律奇异性描述。有效质量倒数的相继截断为零的耦合值（首个即教科书中的失效值α=6）是该半径缓慢收敛的估计。仅由弱耦合系数构造的Padé近似在α直至5的范围内与图解蒙特卡洛结果吻合优于1%。

Conclusion: 基态解析性与强耦合渐近的精确结果意味着系数不可能保持同号，然而所有已知系数均为同号，因此表观奇异性必为靠近正实轴的复共轭对。该奇异性标志着向强耦合的渡越：弱耦合级数能准确定位该渡越，但无法穿过它继续延拓。

Abstract: We calculate the Rayleigh-Schrodinger perturbation series for the ground-state energy and the effective mass of the three-dimensional Frohlich polaron through fourth order in the coupling constant alpha. The third- and fourth-order coefficients of the effective mass are new. Because the Frohlich interaction is a form-bounded perturbation of an isolated nondegenerate ground state at fixed total momentum, the series has a nonzero radius of convergence, and we analyze it with the ratio and Pade methods appropriate to convergent series. The coefficients are strikingly regular and are described by a simple power-law singularity at alpha near 8.5 with an exponent near 1.4. The couplings at which successive truncations of the inverse mass vanish, the first of which is the textbook breakdown value alpha = 6, are slowly converging estimates of this radius. Pade approximants built from the weak-coupling coefficients alone agree with diagrammatic Monte Carlo results for the mass to better than one percent for alpha up to 5. Exact results on the analyticity of the ground state and on the strong-coupling asymptotics imply that the coefficients cannot keep a constant sign, although all known coefficients do, so that the apparent singularity must be a complex-conjugate pair close to the positive real axis. It marks the crossover to strong coupling: the weak-coupling series locates this crossover accurately but cannot be continued through it.

</details>


### [86] [Mapping Strain and Spatial-Modulation Phase from Local Wave Vectors in Scanning Tunneling Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10255)
*Xinze Yang,Sandra Sajan,Maria N. Gastiasoro,Miguel M. Ugeda,Eduardo H. da Silva Neto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出局域波矢映射（LWM）算法，绕过二维相位解缠，直接从实空间测量局域波矢以提取应变与相位梯度，在拓扑缺陷附近稳健，并在模拟与4Ha/2H-NbSe2的STM数据中解析CDW非公度性与相位涡旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STM具备原子级空间分辨能力，可用于局域量化晶格畸变，但传统Lawler–Fujita（LF）方法需对晶格调制相位进行二维解缠，在相位定义不良处（尤其刃位错等拓扑缺陷附近）易失效，因此需要一种不依赖相位解缠的局域应变提取方法。

Method: 提出局域波矢映射（LWM）算法：在实空间直接测量局域波矢，推导局域波矢、应变与相位梯度之间的解析关系，从而无需二维相位解缠即可获得应变与相位梯度分布；并用含已知晶格畸变与人工涡旋的模拟数据做基准测试，随后应用于实验STM数据。

Result: LWM在相位本身定义不良的区域仍保持稳健；基准测试验证了算法在已知畸变与人工涡旋下的准确性；应用于4Ha-NbSe2与2H-NbSe2的实验STM数据，成功解析出CDW非公度性、晶格畸变与CDW相位涡旋，相关物理意义已在S. Sajan, X. Yang等的工作中报道。

Conclusion: LWM为STM局域应变/相位梯度成像提供了稳健替代方案，特别适用于涡旋等拓扑缺陷相关的相位梯度织构；程序包与示例用法已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Spatially varying lattice distortions can produce local changes in electronic properties that are obscured by measurements averaging over extended regions. With its atomic-scale spatial resolution, scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) provides, in principle, a means of quantifying such distortions locally. A widely used approach for extracting local strain from STM images is the Lawler--Fujita (LF) algorithm [Lawler et al., Nature 466, 347 (2010)], which determines the displacement field from the phase of the lattice modulation and subsequently obtains the strain by differentiation. However, this approach requires two-dimensional phase unwrapping, which can fail in regions where the phase is ill-defined, particularly near topological defects such as edge dislocations. Here, we introduce an alternative approach that circumvents these limitations by directly measuring the local wave vector in real space. We derive the relationship between the local wave vector, strain, and the phase gradient, and show that the local wave vector mapping (LWM) algorithm remains robust even when the phase itself is ill-defined. This makes the method particularly well suited for imaging the phase-gradient textures associated with vortices and other topological defects. We benchmark the algorithm using simulated data containing known lattice distortions and artificially generated vortices. Finally, we apply the method to experimental STM data and resolve the charge density wave (CDW) incommensurabilities, lattice distortions, and CDW phase vortices in 4Ha-NbSe2 and 2H-NbSe2. The physical implications of these observations were recently reported by S. Sajan, X. Yang, et al. [arXiv:2607.20335 (2026)]. The program package and the example usage is publicly available at the GitHub: https://github.com/jsntxyang/LocalWavevectorMeasuremnt.

</details>


### [87] [Exact Excitation Spectra and Spectral Gaps of the Frustrated Spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Model via Dual Spin-Sector Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10454)
*Sk Saniur Rahaman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出双自旋扇区VQE框架，通过先在$S_z=1$扇区优化三重态、再用自旋降算符映射回$S_z=0$并施加双扇区惩罚，解决阻挫自旋系统中激发态次序反转问题，实现激发单态的精确求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在阻挫自旋-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$海森堡模型中，常规单扇区方法（如$S_z=0$下的VQD）在$J_2/J_1<0.25$时失效：该区域最低激发态为三重态（$S=1$），其能量低于激发单态（$S=0）。标准deflation会投影掉基态单态后收敛到三重态，误将其识别为激发单态，即激发态次序反转问题。

Method: 提出双自旋扇区VQE框架：首先在$S_z=1$扇区优化基态三重态（该扇区不存在单态），随后通过总自旋降算符$S^-=\sum_i S_i^-$将其映射到$S_z=0$扇区，并构造双扇区惩罚代价函数，从而无变分歧义地隔离激发单态（$E_S$）。在1D自旋链与2D矩形晶格上验证，并开展有限采样模拟与门资源规模分析。

Result: 态矢量数值模拟显示可收敛至机器精度且重叠保真度为单位1。方法成功捕捉关键物理特征：无能隙相、$J_2/J_1\approx0.2411$处的BKT临界点、Majumdar-Ghosh二聚点（$J_2/J_1=0.50$）以及2D非磁阻挫区（$J_2/J_1\approx0.40-0.60$）。有限采样模拟与门规模分析扩展至$N=100$量子比特，验证了算法资源效率与采样鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双自旋扇区VQE框架有效解决了阻挫自旋系统中单扇区算法固有的激发态次序反转问题，可精确提取激发单态并刻画关键量子相特征，且具备面向近期量子硬件的资源效率与采样韧性。

Abstract: Finding excited states and excitation gaps in frustrated spin systems using Variational Quantum Eigensolvers (VQE) is complicated by state-ordering inversions. In the frustrated spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model, standard single-sector algorithms like Variational Quantum Deflation (VQD) in $S_z=0$ fail when $J_2/J_1 < 0.25$. In this regime, the lowest-lying excitation in $S_z=0$ is a Triplet ($S=1$), which lies below the Excited Singlet ($S=0$). Standard deflation projects out the ground singlet and converges onto the Triplet state, misidentifying it as the Excited Singlet. Here, we present a Dual Spin-Sector VQE framework that resolves these state-ordering inversions across 1D spin chains and 2D rectangular lattices. By first optimizing the ground Triplet state in the $S_z=1$ sector (where singlet states cannot exist), mapping it into $S_z=0$ via the total spin-lowering operator $S^- = \sum_i S_i^-$, and evaluating a dual-sector penalty cost function, we isolate the Excited Singlet ($E_S$) without variational ambiguity. Numerical state-vector simulations confirm exact convergence to machine precision with unit overlap fidelity. The method captures key physical features, including the gapless phase, the BKT critical point at $J_2/J_1 \approx 0.2411$, the Majumdar-Ghosh dimer point ($J_2/J_1 = 0.50$), and the 2D non-magnetic frustrated regime ($J_2/J_1 \approx 0.40 - 0.60$). Finite-shot sampling simulations and gate-scaling analytics up to $N=100$ qubits demonstrate the resource efficiency and shot resilience of the algorithm for near-term quantum execution.

</details>


### [88] [Landau Theory for Non-relativistic Magnetism Beyond Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10523)
*Hana Schiff,Judit Romhányi,Paul McClarty,Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将共线磁序的唯象朗道理论推广到共面与非共面磁序，构建所有多维不可约表示通道直至四阶的对称允许自由能，并建立朗道方法与自旋空间群方法之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对非相对论磁系统的朗道理论研究主要局限于共线情形，而共面与非共面磁序在k=0、单一不可约表示下同样普遍存在，缺乏系统的对称性分类与自由能构造，也缺乏与自旋空间群方法的对应关系。

Method: 聚焦零波矢、由晶体点群单一不可约表示描述的全部多维有序通道，按四方、三角/六方、立方对称性分别构造至四阶的对称允许自由能，识别其相变结果，并通过计算净磁化、自旋电导、多极矩、压磁效应等可观测量与自旋空间群约束相对照，辅以各对称性下的具体共面/非共面磁序实例。

Result: 四方、三角/六方与立方对称性给出不同的朗道理论；存在两类自然结果：塌缩为低对称共线相，或形成自旋空间中互相正交的共面/非共面序（类比3He的A、B相）；证明该对称破缺相实现与顺磁相具有相同母空间群的、对应序参量空间表示的自旋空间群；并给出净磁化、自旋电导、多极矩与压磁效应等观测量的结果及其自旋空间群约束。

Conclusion: 朗道方法与自旋空间群方法可建立直接联系；低对称共线相与常规共线自旋群相关，并与原子交替磁性、超流3He及电子向列相存在联系。

Abstract: In this work we study phenomenological Landau theories of non-relativistic magnetic systems, generalizing work on the collinear case to coplanar and non-coplanar orders. Focusing on zero-wavevector ($\boldsymbol{k}=0$) magnetic orders described by a single irreducible representation of the crystallographic point group, we construct the symmetry-allowed free energies to quartic order for all possible multi-dimensional irreducible ordering channels. We find distinct Landau theories for tetragonal, trigonal/hexagonal, and cubic symmetry. Similar to the case of spin-nematic fluids, we find two natural outcomes: a collapse to a lower-symmetry collinear phase or a genuinely coplanar or non-coplanar order where the order parameters form a mutually orthogonal set in spin space -- reminiscent of the $A$ and $B$ phases in ${}^3$He. We show in general that this symmetry-broken phase realizes the spin space group associated with the spatial representation of the order parameter sharing the same parent space group as the paramagnetic phase. This provides a direct link between the Landau and spin space group approaches. We calculate key observables using this Landau approach, including net magnetization, spin conductivity, multipolar moments, and piezomagnetism, connecting our results to constraints from the underlying spin space group. Examples of coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic orders for each case are worked out in detail. Finally, we discuss the relationship of the lower-symmetry collinear phases to the usual collinear spin groups, as well as connections to atomic altermagnetism, superfluid ${}^3$He and electronic nematic phases.

</details>

### [89] [Superconducting edge contact to a buried Ge quantum well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08924)
*Dina Sokolova,Davide Costa,Davide Degli Esposti,Tommaso Costanzo,Giordano Scappucci,Georgios Katsaros,Anton V. Bubis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干法刻蚀与湿法刻蚀结合的边缘接触方案，用于为深埋量子阱制备高质量超导接触，实现了接近理想的界面透明度和安德烈夫反射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体-超导体杂化系统是多种量子现象（如安德烈夫束缚态、拓扑超导、杂化量子霍尔态）的基础；具有深埋量子阱的异质结构具备优异电子特性，可显著提升此类杂化系统性能，但为深埋量子阱建立高质量超导接触仍是难题。

Method: 采用边缘接触方案：先利用各向异性干法刻蚀抵达量子阱，再通过湿法刻蚀进行界面准备，从而在深埋量子阱上形成超导接触，并据此制备约瑟夫森结和量子点接触器件。

Result: 基于该方法制备的约瑟夫森结一致表现出接近单位的界面透明度；量子点接触显示强安德烈夫增强，首个电导平台接近4e²/h（完全安德烈夫反射极限）；在超导量子点接触中还观察到量子化超电流，进一步印证了高接触质量。

Conclusion: 干法与湿法刻蚀相结合是一种稳健策略，可将深埋量子阱集成到超导杂化器件中。

Abstract: Semiconductor-superconductor hybrid systems form the basis for a broad range of quantum phenomena, such as Andreev bound states, topological superconductivity, and hybrid quantum Hall states. The performance of these hybrid systems can strongly benefit from the exceptional electronic properties of heterostructures with deeply buried quantum wells. However, establishing high-quality superconducting contacts to such quantum wells remains challenging. Here, we address this problem with an edge-contact approach that combines anisotropic dry etching to access the quantum well followed by wet etching for interface preparation. Josephson junctions fabricated using this approach consistently exhibit near-unity interface transparency, while quantum point contacts show strong Andreev enhancement, with the first conductance plateau approaching $4e^2/h$, the limit of perfect Andreev reflection. In superconducting quantum point contacts, the high contact quality is further manifested by quantized supercurrent. These results establish the combination of dry and wet etching as a robust strategy for integrating deeply buried quantum wells into superconducting hybrid devices.

</details>


### [90] [Valley transitions during spin shuttling in Si/SiGe heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08984)
*Jonas R F Lima,Guido Burkard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了Si/SiGe异质结构中自旋穿梭时的谷动力学，发现存在一个在数十m/s速度下的区间，即使存在谷激发也能保持量子信息，实现与穿梭距离无关的相干自旋穿梭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谷激发被认为是Si/SiGe异质结构中相干自旋穿梭的最大挑战之一，需要理解穿梭过程中谷动力学并寻找保持量子信息的条件。

Method: 将移动量子点的两个低能谷态视为二能级系统，假设电子初始处于谷基态，分析其在穿梭过程中的演化；并提出基于部分Landau-Zener模型的策略来提升谷穿梭保真度。

Result: 随穿梭速度不同，系统呈现三种不同区间；在数十m/s的穿梭速度下，即使存在谷激发量子信息仍被保留，可实现与穿梭距离无关的相干自旋穿梭，且该区间可抑制自旋-谷热点和空间变化自旋劈裂等退相干机制。基于部分Landau-Zener模型的策略比标准Landau-Zener模型策略更高效。

Conclusion: 在特定穿梭速度下可实现不受距离限制的相干自旋穿梭，结果与近期实现高保真快速度长距离自旋穿梭的实验一致；部分Landau-Zener模型为增强谷穿梭保真度提供了更有效策略。

Abstract: Valley excitations are considered one of the biggest challenges for a coherent spin shuttling in Si/SiGe heterostructures. In this work we theoretically investigate the valley dynamics during spin shuttling in a Si/SiGe heterostructure by considering a two-level system given by the two low-lying valley states of the mobile quantum dot. We assume that the electron is initially in the valley ground state and analyze the evolution of this state during shuttling. We find that, depending on the shuttling velocity, the system can be in three distinct regimes. In one of these regimes, the quantum information is preserved even in the presence of the valley excitations, allowing for coherent spin shuttling independently of the shuttling distance. This regime is obtained for a shuttling velocity of tens of m/s, a desired regime where dephasing mechanisms, such as spin-valley hotspots and spatially varying spin splitting, can be suppressed. This result is in accordance with recent experimental work that obtained a high spin shuttling fidelity after a fast and long shuttling. We also propose strategies based on the Partial Landau-Zener model to enhance the valley shuttling fidelity, which was found to be much more efficient than previously proposed strategies based on the standard Landau-Zener model.

</details>


### [91] [Momentum-Locked Edge Excitons in 2D Molecular Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09148)
*Sidhartha Nayak,Alex Boeglin,Quentin Stein,Katharina Kaiser,Guillaume Schull,Thomas Frederiksen,Tomáš Neuman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究二维平面分子晶体中双重简并激发的Frenkel激子，发现其可形成具有非平凡拓扑特征的激子能带，且边缘激子仅在特定晶向边界出现，由体带Zak相位决定，并伴随动量锁定的椭圆偏振织构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体中分子激发可杂化为集体Frenkel激子，但其在二维体系中的拓扑性质及边界依赖性尚不清晰，需要阐明边缘态存在条件及其对晶向的敏感性。

Abstract: Collective molecular excitations govern energy transport in light-harvesting and optoelectronic materials. Here we use scanning tunnelling microscopy-induced luminescence to image Frenkel excitons in two-dimensional zinc phthalocyanine molecular crystals with nanometer resolution. We identify an excitonic state localized at the crystal perimeter and show, through comparison with microscopic theory, that it originates from the momentum-resolved non-trivial topology of the bulk excitonic bands. Our results establish molecular crystals as a platform for engineering topological excitonic states and provide a strategy for controlling energy transport through excitonic band engineering.

Conclusion: 研究阐明了空间非局域性在范德华异质结构极化激元特性中的基础性作用，为纳米光子学中光局域与能量转移调控提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Achieving nanoscale light confinement and long-range propagation is a central challenge in nanophotonics, with important implications for controlling molecular photophysical processes and energy transfer. Monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) support in-plane exciton-polaritons (IPEPs), deeply subwavelength optical modes in the visible range. In this regime, electromagnetic fields vary on length scales comparable to the exciton size, making spatial nonlocality in the excitonic optical response essential. Here, we develop a microscopic theory of the nonlocal excitonic optical response and investigate the near-field properties of IPEPs in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN)-encapsulated monolayer WS$_2$. Combining a two-band massive Dirac fermion description with a generalized Mott-Wannier treatment, we derive a nonlocal electric susceptibility that accounts for both the center-of-mass and internal relative motion of excitons. Spatial nonlocality in the excitonic optical response shifts the polariton dispersion toward lower in-plane wavevectors compared with the results based on the local-response approximation. This reduces high-wavevector contributions and produces smoother near-field distributions that extend over larger lateral distances while retaining nanoscale confinement. Furthermore, at higher excitation energies, multiple exciton-polariton modes contribute at distinct wavevectors, and their relative contributions can be tuned by varying the thickness of the capping hBN layer. These insights elucidate the fundamental role of spatial nonlocality in the polaritonic properties of van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>

Method: 制备完全悬浮的无定形MoGe超导纳米线，栅极与纳米线之间以真空隔离，从而消除所有漏电流驱动通道；测量相滑移逃逸率Γ随栅压的变化，并用多种逃逸率模型进行拟合。

Result: 观察到栅压诱导的Γ大幅增强，在六个数量级范围内呈指数关系；拟合结果显示与简单相滑移模型存在明显偏差；且Γ在栅压极性反转时并不保持不变。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了栅压与相滑移逃逸率Γ之间清晰且非平凡的关联，提示静电场与相滑移中心的复杂相互作用，为后续探究该效应起源的研究提供了方向。

Abstract: A gate voltage can suppress the critical current of a superconducting nanowire, and various scenarios have been proposed to describe these observations. The key difficulty is that these scenarios, including both leakage-driven and field-driven mechanisms, are impossible to disentangle in standard experiments. Here, we resolve this issue by studying a fully suspended superconducting nanowire of amorphous MoGe, separated from the gate by vacuum, eliminating all leakage-driven channels. We measure the phase slip escape rate $Γ$ and find a large gate-induced enhancement of $Γ$, exponential over six orders of magnitude. Fitting various escape-rate models reveals a strong deviation from simple phase slip models, hinting at a complex interaction between the electrostatic field and the phase slip centers. Surprisingly, $Γ$ is not invariant under reversal of the gate-voltage polarity. Our work establishes a clear and nontrivial connection between gate voltage and $Γ$, guiding future research into the origins of the effect.

### [95] [Self-induced spin-orbit torque switching in a synthetic antiferromagnetic Co 2 MnGa /MnGa bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09885)

Abstract: The large intrinsic spin current in magnetic Weyl semimetals (WSMs) provides a promising platform for spin-orbit torque (SOT) devices. Here, we demonstrate SOTdriven magnetization switching in a synthetic antiferromagnet (SAF) composed of a Co2MnGa(CMG)/MnGa bilayer. In this heavy metal-free structure, CMG film functions simultaneously as a spin current source and as a magnetic layer. Macrospin simulations confirm the self-induced switching mechanism in the bilayer. The SOT generated by CMG switches the MnGa layer, which in turn triggers the reversal of the CMG layer via strong antiferromagnetic exchange coupling. These results elucidate the spin dynamics in SAFs and provide a new pathway toward SOT devices with selfinduced magnetization switching.

</details>


### [96] [Construction-Dependent Floquet Topology in Circularly Driven $N$-Stacked Su-Schrieffer-Heeger Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09925)
*Aayushi Agrawal,Jayendra N. Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在二维SSH链堆叠晶格中，研究了依赖于"构建方式"的Floquet拓扑。两种静态极限下幺正等价的构建在圆偏振驱动下因Peierls相位差异而呈现不同的Floquet相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态下幺正等价的两种构建方式在周期驱动下是否仍等价，是Floquet拓扑研究中的核心问题。本文旨在揭示构成链的物理构建方式（即物理键的方向）对驱动系统拓扑结构的决定性作用。

Method: 构建两种二维晶格：由拓扑平庸（w=0）和平庸非平庸（w=1）的SSH链堆叠，子格轨道共位。施加圆偏振驱动，采用Floquet-Sambe形式计算准能谱与相图（下能带Chern数C_L与方向Zak相位Z_x, Z_y），并计算动态缠绕数W_0、W_π及手征边缘态；进一步计算时间平均光学Hall电导。

Result: 两种构建呈现不同的Floquet相结构，包括低频C_L=-3与C_L=-4相。Chern数无法分辨两个准能隙的拓扑，动态缠绕数可区分：相同驱动参数下C_L=-2，但(W_0,W_π)分别为(0,+2)和(-2,0)。时间平均光学Hall电导的直流值为C_L e²/h（理想满带占据），有限频响应含光子辅助贡献。

Conclusion: 构成链的物理构建方式虽在静态体谱中无关紧要，却对Floquet拓扑结构起决定性作用。该结果凸显了周期驱动系统中几何/物理实现细节的重要性，并讨论了驱动模型的实验实现方案。

Abstract: We investigate construction-dependent Floquet topology in a two-dimensional lattice formed by stacking Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains with co-located sublattice orbitals. We present two constructions in the decoupled-chain limit. Construction-I: the constituent SSH chains are topologically trivial with winding number $w=0$. Construction-II: the constituent SSH chains are topologically nontrivial with $w=1$. Although these constructions are unitarily equivalent in the static limit, circular driving distinguishes them because the hopping processes related by the transformation correspond to different physical bond vectors and therefore acquire different Peierls phases. Using the Floquet-Sambe formalism, we determine the quasienergy spectra and phase diagrams in terms of the lower-band Chern number $C_L$ and directional Zak phases $(Z_x,\, Z_y)$. We find distinct Floquet phase structures, including low-frequency phases with $C_L = -3$ and $C_L = -4$ in the two constructions. Since the Chern number does not resolve the topology of the two quasienergy gaps separately, we further calculate the dynamical winding numbers $W_0$ and $W_π$, and verify the corresponding chiral edge states. In particular, at identical driving parameters, both constructions can have the same Chern numbers $C_L=-2$, while their gap-resolved winding numbers are $(W_0,\, W_π)=(0,\,+2)$ and $(-2,\,0)$, respectively. We also calculate the time-averaged optical Hall conductivity and show that $ C_Le^2/h$ determines its dc value for ideal full-band occupation. At the same time, its finite-frequency response contains photon-assisted contributions. Our results demonstrate that the physical construction of the constituent chains, although irrelevant to the static bulk spectrum, can play a decisive role in the Floquet topological structure. We discuss possible experimental realizations of the driven model.

</details>


### [97] [Programmable Asymmetric Spin-Orbit Torque Switching for Spin Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09950)
*Rongkun Han,Dahai Wei,Jianhua Zhao,Yuan Lu*

Motivation: 自旋逻辑器件有望实现超低功耗、非易失的信息处理，但需要阐明非对称磁化翻转的物理起源并探索可重构逻辑的实现途径。

Method: 结合实验与宏自旋模拟，研究面内磁各向异性与垂直磁各向异性异质结构中无外场自旋轨道力矩驱动的非对称磁化翻转。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了非对称磁化翻转的物理机制，并提出了一种实现可重构自旋逻辑器件的途径。

Abstract: Spin logic devices provide a promising route toward ultralow-power and nonvolatile information processing. In this work, we demonstrate field-free spin-orbit torque-induced asymmetric magnetization switching in in-plane anisotropy (IMA) and perpendicular magnetic anisotropy heterostructures, characterized by different critical switching currents under opposite current polarities. Combined experiments and macrospin simulations reveal that the asymmetric switching originates from an in-plane effective field Hy , which breaks the Myz mirror symmetry of the system. By controlling the magnetization direction of the IMA layer, both the switching polarity and bias direction can be tuned. Building on four distinct types of asymmetric switching behaviors, we realize a reconfigurable spin logic operation within a single Hall-bar device. These results clarify the physical origin of asymmetric magnetization switching and demonstrate an approach for realizing reconfigurable spin logic devices.

</details>


### [98] [Topological and kinetic origins of fractional thermal conductance at the topological insulator--superconductor interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10011)
*Laurence A. Jacobs,Guillermo R. Zemba*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一个定理：在完全热平衡下，由理想浮动接触点连接的手性边缘段网络的双端热导仅是各段净手性中心荷的有理函数，且对局部边界动力学不变。其推论是一个不可能性陈述：若两种实现向同一网络提供相同的异常数据，则任何热导测量都无法区分它们。作者进一步以拓扑绝缘体-超导体界面为应用，通过洛伦兹比与过剩散粒噪声来分辨机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在双层石墨烯双极(ν,ν')=(2,-1)结观测到稳健的半整数热导平台κ_0 T/2，但其来源并非非阿贝尔拓扑，而是共传播电子与空穴边缘模式的完全平衡。这一巧合引出一个关键问题：何时能够从热导测量中区分半整数平台的不同物理起源？论文旨在给出判定两种起源能否被区分的普适判据。

Method: 作者证明了一个定理：在完全热平衡条件下，由理想浮动接触点连接的手性边缘段网络，其双端热导是各段净手性中心荷的仅含这些量的有理函数；这些中心荷由引力异常钉定，并在任意局部边界动力学下不变。基于此定理得出推论——只要两种实现对同一网络给出相同的异常数据，热导测量便无法区分二者。随后以拓扑绝缘体-超导体界面为具体应用展开分析。

Result: (2,-1)完全平衡值与手性马约拉纳模式中心荷相等，只是某一填充组合下的算术事实；凡是出现此类巧合之处，热测量均无法触及，区分必须来自电荷部门，而电荷部门在超导边界处不被异常钉定。在拓扑绝缘体-超导体界面应用中：涡旋因θ=π磁电耦合携带分数电荷e/4，边界承载中心荷为1/2的手性马约拉纳模式，侧向相邻的整数量子霍尔区提供动力学实现。该复合器件的洛伦兹比为L_0/(1+4|ν_{IQH}|)（孤立马约拉纳边界时为反常），过剩散粒噪声随B增长（源自e/4涡旋隧穿）而非锁定于量子霍尔平台，二者共同分辨机制——前提是量子霍尔边缘不与超导体相接。

Conclusion: 热导测量在异常数据相同的情况下无法区分半整数平台的拓扑与非拓扑起源，因为完全平衡下双端热导仅由异常钉定的中心荷决定。拓扑绝缘体-超导体界面提供了通过电荷部门分辨机制的途径：洛伦兹比与过剩散粒噪声可作为判别信号，但要求量子霍尔边缘不与超导体相接。

Abstract: A recent experiment by Roy et al. (Nat. Commun. 17, 2853 (2026)) demonstrated a robust half-integer thermal conductance plateau, κ_0 T/2, at a bipolar (ν,ν')=(2,-1) junction in bilayer graphene, produced not by non-Abelian topology but by full equilibration of co-propagating electron and hole edge modes. We prove a theorem that fixes when the two origins can be told apart: under full thermal equilibration, the two-terminal thermal conductance of a network of chiral edge segments joined at ideal floating contacts is a rational function of the net chiral central charges of the segments alone, quantities pinned by the gravitational anomaly and invariant under arbitrary local boundary kinetics. The corollary is an impossibility statement: whenever two realizations present the same anomaly data to the same network, no thermal-conductance measurement can distinguish them. That the equilibrated (2,-1) value equals the central charge of a chiral Majorana mode is an arithmetic fact about one filling combination, but wherever such a coincidence occurs it is beyond the reach of thermometry, and the separation must come from the charge sector, which is not anomaly-pinned at a superconducting boundary. We develop the topological insulator-superconductor interface as the application: a vortex carries fractional charge e/4 from the θ= πmagnetoelectric coupling, the boundary hosts a chiral Majorana mode of central charge 1/2, and a laterally adjacent integer quantum Hall (IQH) region supplies the kinetic realization. The Lorenz ratio (anomalous for the isolated Majorana boundary, L_0 / (1 + 4|ν_{IQH}|) in the composite device) and the excess shot noise (growing with B from e/4 vortex tunneling versus locked to IQH plateaus) together resolve the mechanism, provided the quantum Hall edge does not abut the superconductor.

</details>


### [99] [Coupled quantum critical states in a circuit simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10048)

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了耦合Kondo任意子的命运，通过玻色化、量子布朗运动与数值重整化群方法，得到了完整的准弹道相图与低温输运性质，揭示了具有连续可调输运与磁化率指数的稳健非费米液体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合金属-半导体量子电路为研究强关联量子物态提供了可控平台，但耦合临界态能否组合成新的非费米液体相这一问题仍待解答。受近期两格点电荷-Kondo电路实验实现的启发，作者旨在探究从每个格点的双通道Kondo临界点产生的耦合Kondo任意子的行为。

Method: 作者采用玻色化与量子布朗运动方法获得了完整的准弹道相图和低温输运性质，同时利用数值重整化群计算解析了互补的弱耦合极限。

Result: 两种方法共同提供了统一描述，揭示了具有连续可调输运指数与磁化率指数的稳健非费米液体相；更强的格点间耦合最终产生被屏蔽的费米液体。

Conclusion: 耦合Kondo任意子可形成具有连续可调指数的稳健非费米液体相，而增强格点间耦合会导致相变为被屏蔽的费米液体，为量子模拟强关联物态提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Hybrid metal-semiconductor quantum circuits offer a controlled setting for realizing and probing strongly correlated quantum matter. A largely open question that may be addressed by such simulators is whether coupled critical states can combine into new non-Fermi liquid (NFL) phases. Inspired by the recent realization of a two-site charge-Kondo circuit [W. Pouse \textit{et al.}, \href{https://www.nature.com/articles/s41567-022-01905-4}{Nat. Phys. \textbf{19}, 492 (2023)}], we uncover the fate of coupled Kondo anyons emerging from the two-channel-Kondo critical point at each site. Bosonization and quantum Brownian motion methods yield the full quasiballistic phase diagram and low-temperature transport properties, while numerical renormalization group calculations resolve the complementary weak-coupling limit. Together, these provide a unified description, and reveal robust NFL phases with continuously tunable transport and susceptibility exponents. Stronger inter-site coupling ultimately produces a screened Fermi liquid.

</details>


### [100] [Charge transport in two-dimensional conductors with hybrid three-component plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10060)
*A. D. Levin,V. A. Chitta,Z. D. Kvon,N. N. Mikhailov,G. M. Gusev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究无间隙HgTe量子阱中多组分电子-空穴等离子体的输运，发现近电中性时电阻随T²增加，源于狄拉克电子与热激活重空穴的散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子-空穴等离子体此前主要研究两类极限：具有抛物线的简并半金属和石墨烯这类非简并对称狄拉克体系，而由无质量狄拉克载流子与非简并重质量载流子共存的多组分体系尚缺乏研究。

Method: 在无间隙HgTe量子阱中结合自洽能带计算得到的载流子密度，采用短程电子-空穴相互作用与弱无序极限下的弛豫时间近似，分析近电中性处的输运行为。

Result: 近电中性时电阻近似按T²增加，与对称狄拉克等离子体近温度无关的电阻率形成鲜明对比；理论和数据表明电中性使化学势钉扎在狄拉克点之上，狄拉克电子适度简并而重空穴保持非简并并服从玻尔兹曼统计，轻电子与重空穴近似弹性散射，短程相互作用下电导率按T⁻²变化且过剩电阻率正比于重空穴密度，而无屏蔽库仑相互作用则给出温度无关的电导率，提取的相互作用幅度符合库仑尺度。

Conclusion: 近临界HgTe量子阱可作为多组分体系中相互作用驱动输运的研究平台，该体系结合了简并无质量载流子与非简并大质量载流子。

Abstract: Electron--hole plasma in two-dimensional systems has mainly been studied in two limiting cases: degenerate semimetals with parabolic bands and nondegenerate symmetric Dirac systems such as graphene. Here we investigate a different regime realized in a gapless HgTe quantum well: a multicomponent plasma where massless Dirac carriers coexist with thermally activated heavy holes from lateral valence-band valleys. Near charge neutrality, the resistance increases approximately as $T^2$, in sharp contrast to the nearly temperature-independent resistivity expected for a symmetric Dirac plasma. We show that once heavy holes are populated, charge neutrality pins the chemical potential above the Dirac point, leaving the Dirac electrons moderately degenerate while the heavy holes remain nondegenerate and obey Boltzmann statistics. The light electrons therefore scatter almost elastically from the heavy holes, allowing a relaxation-time treatment of interparticle transport. Using carrier densities from self-consistent band-structure calculations, we reproduce the data with a short-range electron--hole interaction in the weakly disordered limit, for which the conductivity scales as $T^{-2}$ and the excess resistivity is proportional to the heavy-hole density. In contrast, an unscreened Coulomb interaction would give a temperature-independent conductivity. The extracted interaction amplitude is of the expected Coulomb scale. These results establish near-critical HgTe quantum wells as a platform for interaction-driven transport in multicomponent systems combining degenerate massless and nondegenerate massive carriers.

</details>


### [101] [Gentle Floquet control of orbital Hall effect and orbital inverse Faraday effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10109)
*Pei-Han Lin,Roderich Moessner,Egor I. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在相空间约束强的系统中，Floquet 驱动的相对效应被共振增强，可在较弱驱动下显著改变轨道霍尔电导，并通过圆偏振驱动诱导逆轨道法拉第效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet 工程通过时间周期电磁驱动调控材料响应，但实际应用的关键难题是降低所需驱动强度。作者希望寻找能够以较弱驱动实现显著 Floquet 效应的机制。

Method: 作者考虑具有强相空间约束的系统，其响应由动量空间中很小的区域主导。以轨道霍尔效应为例：平衡占据将带间相干限制在窄动量区间 ħΔk 内，分析共振 Floquet 驱动的相对影响，并研究关键比值 eE₀/(Ω₀ħΔk)（Ω₀ 为驱动频率，E₀ 为电场振幅）。此外分析圆偏振驱动在非平凡轨道织构下诱导的逆轨道法拉第效应及其磁化率。

Result: 即使在场振幅 E₀ 不大的情况下，关键比值 eE₀/(Ω₀ħΔk) 也相当可观，导致轨道霍尔电导发生大的相对变化。圆偏振驱动通过磁化电子诱导出逆轨道法拉第效应，其磁化率由决定共振 Floquet 杂化的带间量子度规控制。

Conclusion: 强相空间约束可增强共振 Floquet 驱动的相对效应，从而在适度驱动强度下实现显著的轨道霍尔电导调控；带间量子度规是控制逆轨道法拉第效应磁化率的关键量，为低功耗 Floquet 器件设计提供思路。

Abstract: Floquet engineering controls material responses via a time-periodic electromagnetic drive. An important task in practise is to reduce the driving strengths required to implement Floquet protocols.We show that in systems with strong phase-space constraints, where the response is dominated by a small region of momentum space, the relative impact of a resonant Floquet drive is enhanced. We demonstrate this idea on the example of the orbital Hall effect, where equilibrium occupations restrict the interband coherence to a narrow momentum interval $\hbarΔk$. The ratio $eE_0/(Ω_0\hbarΔk)$, where $Ω_0$ is the frequency of the drive, is considerable even at modest field amplitudes $E_0$, leading to a large relative change in the orbital Hall conductivity. Due to the non-trivial orbital texture, the circularly polarized drive induces an inverse orbital Faraday effect by magnetizing the electrons. We show that the corresponding susceptibility is controlled by the interband quantum metric that determines the resonant Floquet hybridization.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Equilibrium Raman Thermometry: Fermi-Level Control of the Anti-Stokes Response in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10159)
*Martin Jindra,Luka Pirker,Tim Verhagen,Ciarán Fowley,Martin Kalbáč,Matěj Velický,† Riichiro Saito,Ado Jorio,Otakar Frank*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过门控反斯托克斯拉曼光谱发现单层石墨烯的反斯托克斯响应偏离平衡声子布居预期，G模反斯托克斯强度随费米能级偏离狄拉克点被强烈且可逆地抑制，而斯托克斯强度基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡电子与声子布居主导纳米器件的产热与散热，但直接表征困难。反斯托克斯拉曼散射是探测声子布居和局域温度的灵敏手段，但当电子与声子布居偏离平衡时其解释变得复杂，因此需要研究门控下的反斯托克斯响应机制。

Method: 采用空间局域微液滴光谱电化学平台、介电背栅和化学掺杂，对单层石墨烯进行门控依赖的反斯托克斯拉曼测量，比较G模斯托克斯与反斯托克斯支的强度、频率和线宽随掺杂的演化。

Result: 随费米能级偏离狄拉克点，G模反斯托克斯强度出现强烈且可逆的抑制，斯托克斯强度基本不变；反斯托克斯/斯托克斯强度比在测量范围内下降数倍，并表现出显著的门控依赖功率响应，在电荷中性点灵敏度最高。而G模频率和线宽随掺杂的演化符合已建立的Kohn反常图像。

Conclusion: 反斯托克斯拉曼散射可作为探测石墨烯中电子-声子耦合和非平衡声子动力学的灵敏探针；结果提示费米能级依赖的电子相空间起重要作用，影响热载流子驱动的声子布居及电子介导的斯托克斯-反斯托克斯散射。

Abstract: Non-equilibrium electron and phonon populations govern heat generation and dissipation in nanoscale devices, yet their direct characterization remains challenging. Anti-Stokes Raman scattering provides a sensitive probe of phonon populations and local temperatures, although its interpretation becomes more complex when electronic and phononic populations are driven out of equilibrium. Here, we investigate the gate-dependent anti-Stokes Raman response of monolayer graphene and find that the anti-Stokes response deviates from that expected from an equilibrium phonon population. Using a spatially localized microdroplet spectroelectrochemical platform, dielectric back-gates, and chemical doping, we observe a strong and reversible suppression of the G-mode anti-Stokes intensity as the Fermi level is tuned away from the Dirac point, while the Stokes intensity remains essentially unchanged. By contrast, the G-mode frequency and linewidth of both the Stokes and anti-Stokes branches evolve with doping as expected from the established Kohn-anomaly picture. The anti-Stokes/Stokes intensity ratio decreases by several-fold over the measured range and displays a pronounced, gate-dependent power response with maximum sensitivity at charge neutrality. Comparison with previous studies of non-equilibrium carrier dynamics in doped graphene suggests an important role of Fermi-level-dependent electronic phase space, which can influence both hot-carrier-driven phonon populations and electronically mediated Stokes-anti-Stokes scattering. Our findings establish anti-Stokes Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of electron-phonon coupling and non-equilibrium phonon dynamics in graphene.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum-vortex excitons beyond band topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10175)
*Jin-Hyung Choi,Sang-Hoon Han,Young-Kwon Han,Jun-Won Rhim,Sun-Woo Kim,Joshua J. P. Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了在平带Chern带中，库仑相位匹配可使量子涡旋激子成为最低能束缚态，并给出激子拓扑与光学响应的微观联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维半导体通常以无节点的s型激子为最低能束缚态，而在平带Chern带中是否存在拓扑非平凡的激子基态尚不清楚，且激子拓扑与库仑束缚、光学选择定则之间的关系缺乏微观理解。

Method: 以典型的Yin-Yang Kagome晶格为模型，通过调控自旋轨道耦合强度驱动激子态转变；引入由激子相位差与Bloch重叠相位组成的规范不变量，比较具有相同波函数幅度的态以分离纯相位贡献，并依据相对导带-价带Chern数差的动量空间波函数缠绕数对激子拓扑进行分类。

Result: 随着自旋轨道耦合增强，最低激子从量子涡旋态转变为零缠绕态，而能带Chern数保持不变；该相位贡献足以驱动激子能级次序反转；不同拓扑态对应不同的圆偏振选择定则，光学吸收谱中表现为从暗涡旋激子到亮零缠绕激子的转变。

Conclusion: 研究建立了激子拓扑、库仑束缚与光学响应之间的微观联系，表明激子物理所携带的信息超出了底层电子能带Chern数的内容。

Abstract: Optically exciting a typical low-dimensional semiconductor produces a nodeless \(s\)-type exciton as its lowest-energy bound state. Here, we explain how Coulomb phase matching stabilises a quantum-vortex exciton as the lowest bound state in flat Chern bands. Using a prototypical Yin--Yang kagome lattice, we demonstrate that increasing the spin--orbit coupling drives a transition of the lowest exciton from a quantum-vortex state to a zero-winding state, while leaving the band Chern numbers unchanged. We trace this behaviour to a gauge-invariant combination of exciton phase differences and Bloch-overlap phases that can reduce the Coulomb energy. By comparing states with identical wavefunction amplitudes, we isolate this phase contribution and show that it is sufficient to drive the reversal in exciton ordering. We classify exciton topology by the momentum-space wavefunction winding relative to the conduction--valence Chern-number difference,connecting the competing states to circular-polarisation selection rules. We then show that the resulting reordering can be tracked directly in the optical absorption spectrum, through the transition from a dark vortex exciton to a bright zero-winding exciton. These findings establish a microscopic link between exciton topology, Coulomb binding, and optical response that extends beyond the information contained in the underlying electronic band Chern numbers.

</details>


### [104] [Deterministic Vortex Generation from Coupled Trapped Polariton Condensate Triad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10251)
*X. Qin,N. Pitanios,S. Dam,S. Betzold,S. Höfling,S. V. Koniakhin,P. G. Savvidis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在三个耦合的激子-极化激元凝聚体中,通过调节非共振泵浦功率控制耗散耦合符号,确定性地生成并全光开关三种拓扑不同的涡旋态,并从动量空间发射分布直接读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种无需干涉测量、无需共振相位或OAM印记即可实现手性/OAM按需控制与可重构手性光源的紧凑平台。

Method: 三元捕获激子-极化激元凝聚体构成的凝聚体三重态;调节非共振泵浦功率以改变耗散耦合符号;使用控制光束按需激活态;通过动量空间光致发光分布进行态读数。

Result: 低功率下为铁磁同相态(零绕数,远场低动量无结构,在Dirac点形成涡旋-反涡旋阵列,体现C_3v对称性);两个阻挫态具有±2π绕数,可选择性地在K或K'点产生不同的三角形发射图案。三种态实空间分布相同,但动量空间分布可明确区分手性。

Conclusion: 该方案实现了基于泵浦的轨道角动量按需控制,不依赖共振相位或OAM印记,将凝聚体三重态确立为以三态逻辑运作为基础的可重构手性光生成紧凑平台。

Abstract: We present the deterministic generation and all-optical switching of ternary topologically distinct vortex states in a triad of trapped exciton-polariton condensates with direct state readout from momentum-space emission distribution. Tuning the non-resonant pump power controls the sign of the dissipative coupling, selecting between a ferromagnetic in-phase state and two frustrated states with global phase winding. The zero-winding ferromagnetic state has unstructured far-field emission at low momenta and forms vortex-antivortex arrays at the Dirac points, reflecting the C_3v global symmetry. The two frustrated states of +-2pi winding can be activated on-demand by a control beam and produce different triangular emission patterns selectively localized at the K or K' points. Although all three states share identical real-space emission distributions, their momentum-space photoluminescence profiles distinguish them unambiguously, revealing the chirality without the need for interferometry. This enables on-demand orbital angular momentum control using a pump independent of resonant phase or OAM imprinting, establishing condensate triads as a compact platform for reconfigurable chiral light generation operating on a ternary logic basis.

</details>


### [105] [Exciton switching and tunable exciton-phonon coupling in Oxygen doped ZnO nanorods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10353)
*Aiswarya Rath,Kalyan Ghosh,Pratap K Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过250 keV氧离子辐照垂直取向ZnO纳米棒，可逆调控激子发光（OFF/ON），并协同调制激子-声子耦合与回音壁模式共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自离子掺杂是动态调控ZnO纳米棒激子态与光-物质相互作用的有效途径，但辐照诱导缺陷与激子及光学响应之间的直接关联尚不明确，需要建立系统联系。

Method: 对垂直取向六方ZnO纳米棒进行250 keV氧离子辐照，改变离子注量；结合变温及低温光致发光光谱，分析近带边发射、缺陷带发射与激子-声子耦合，并辅以热退火实现可逆性验证。

Result: 随注量增加，380 nm近带边发射逐渐猝灭直至激子完全OFF，热退火后恢复，实现可逆OFF/ON；400–750 nm缺陷带出现准周期回音壁模式类共振，并随辐照改变纳米棒几何与光限制而逐渐抑制；低温光谱分辨出D0X、FX-1LO、FX-2LO跃迁，温度演化符合Bose-Einstein模型；激子-声子耦合强度呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 氧离子辐照可同时工程化激子开关、激子-声子相互作用与光学共振，为缺陷可编程的ZnO纳米光子与光电器件提供路径。

Abstract: Self ion doping in ZnO nanorods offers a powerful route to dynamically control excitonic states and light matter interactions. Here, vertically aligned hexagonal ZnO nanorods were irradiated with 250 keV oxygen ions to establish a direct link between irradiation induced defects and excitonic and optical responses. Increasing ion fluence progressively quenches the near band edge emission at 380 nm, leading to a complete exciton OFF state, while thermal annealing restores the emission, yielding a reversible exciton OFF or ON response. This switching originates from defect mediated modification and thermal recovery of excitonic states. The defect band emission, between 400 to 750 nm, exhibits quasi periodic whispering gallery mode like resonances that are progressively suppressed as irradiation alters nanorod geometry and optical confinement. Low-temperature photoluminescence resolves D0X, FX 1LO, and FX 2LO transitions, whose temperature evolution follows the Bose Einstein model. The non-monotonic variation of excition phonon coupling strength reveals a competition among irradiation induced defects, lattice distortion, and exciton localization in determining exciton phonon coupling. These results establish Oxygen ion irradiation as a means to simultaneously engineer excitonic switching, exciton phonon interactions, and optical resonances, providing a pathway toward defect programmable ZnO nanophotonic and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [106] [Pseudospin Hall Transport Induced by Berry Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10376)
*Qinhui Jiang,Jidong Song,Qingyang Mo,Bo Li,Dongyi Wang,Shuang Zhang,Mengyao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文揭示了赝自旋-1狄拉克体系中贝里曲率的“面内极性”这一新几何自由度，并证明其与实空间质量梯度的定向耦合决定无隙赝自旋霍尔模的涌现，通过声学实验验证了方向选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统狄拉克锥具有的平带交叉与非阿贝尔特性使赝自旋-1狄拉克体系具有独特物理，但其拓扑输运性质在被动、时间反演不变情形下长期未被充分探索；同时，净贝里通量为零时贝里曲率的符号分辨分布这一几何自由度亦未被关注。

Method: 构建赝自旋-1狄拉克哈密顿量，分析贝里曲率纹理的面内极性；通过设计四格点平面晶格的胞内耦合，在不改变母晶格对称性的前提下独立编程贝里曲率极性与空间质量梯度；并以声学实验进行验证。

Result: 实验证实了定向选择规则：反转质量梯度可关闭或重新打开色散隙；赝自旋选择性源激发可沿任意指定轴发射反向传播的赝自旋支。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子几何极性确立为可重构波导、传感及高容量量子应用的通用工具。

Abstract: Pseudospin-1 Dirac systems exhibit unique physics distinct from conventional Dirac cones, such as flat-band crossings and non-Abelian characteristics, yet their topological transport properties have remained largely untapped in passive, time-reversal-invariant settings. Here we uncover an in-plane polarity of the Berry-curvature texture in a pseudospin-1 Dirac Hamiltonian, a previously unexplored geometric degree of freedom encoded in the sign-resolved distribution of Berry curvature despite zero net Berry flux, and reveal a new mechanics where Berry curvature induce pseudospin Hall behaviors in a system. We show that the oriented coupling between this momentum-space polarity and a real-space mass gradient governs a geometric selection rule that dictates the emergence of gapless pseudospin Hall modes. By engineering the intracell couplings of a four-site planar lattice, we independently program the Berry-curvature polarity and the spatial mass gradient without altering the host lattice symmetry. Acoustic experiments directly confirm this directional selection rule: reversing the mass gradient closes or reopens the dispersive gap, while pseudospin-selective source excitation launches counterpropagating pseudospin branches along arbitrary prescribed axes. Our work establishes quantum geometric polarity as a versatile tool for reconfigurable wave routing, sensing, and high-capacity quantum applications.

</details>

### [107] [Matter wave bistability with a momentum chirped Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08942)
*Mikhail Mamaev,Nicholas Mantella,Noah P. Baker,Joseph McGowan,Aephraim M. Steinberg*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文理论提出：通过对BEC波包施加动量啁啾，平衡腔内动力学平均场能量移动，可克服相互作用导致的透射峰展宽，实现物质波Fabry-Perot干涉中的增强共振谱与双稳特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEC中的原子间碰撞引入非线性效应（如多值透射谱），但相互作用导致的腔共振移动会使透射峰展宽，因此物质波FP干涉中相互作用效应的实验观测尚未实现。作者希望突破这一限制。

Method: 理论分析：在BEC波包中工程化动量啁啾，使其与透射过程中腔的动力学平均场能量移动相平衡；采用准一维冷原子BEC与由一对光诱导高斯势垒构成的腔进行碰撞的模型；并基准化所需最优啁啾、表征相互作用共振处近不连续FP透射谱的对比度。

Result: 预测通过啁啾平衡可获得增强的共振谱以及由相互作用辅助隧穿引起的双稳特性，这些在原本条件下无法实现；该物理在准一维BEC与光诱导高斯双势垒腔的体系中可直接观测。

Conclusion: 该方案可实现物质波开关协议与高精度速度测量等技术，并给出了后者可达灵敏度的估计。

Abstract: While a Fabry-Perot (FP) cavity interferometer is a standard tool in modern optics, analogous physics can also be observed with matter waves such as Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs). Interatomic collisions in BECs can induce nonlinear features such as multi-valued transmission spectra for interferometry experiments. Observation of interaction effects in the matter-wave analogue of FP interferometry has not yet been experimentally achieved, mostly due to broadening of transmission peaks by interaction-induced shifts of cavity resonances. In this work, we theoretically show that this limitation can be overcome by engineering a momentum chirp in a BEC wavepacket. By balancing this chirp against the dynamical mean-field energy shift of the cavity during transmission, we predict enhanced resonant spectra and bistability features due to interaction-assisted tunneling that would be otherwise inaccessible. We show that this physics is readily observable with quasi-one-dimensional BECs of cold atoms colliding with a pair of light-induced Gaussian potential barriers forming the cavity. We also benchmark the requisite optimum chirp needed and characterize the contrast of near-discontinuous FP transmission spectra at an interacting resonance. Our findings enable the realization of technologies like matter-wave switching protocols, and high-precision velocity measurements; estimates of achievable sensitivity for the latter are provided.

</details>


### [108] [Quench dynamics and quantum flutter properties of one-dimensional attractive single-spin flipped Fermi gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09431)
*Xiangguo Yin,Shulong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用Bethe ansatz精确解研究一维理想费米气体中带初始动量的吸引型自旋向下杂质的非平衡动力学，揭示弱吸引下的束缚态与混合振荡行为、强吸引下的局域束缚态主导动力学及Friedel-like振荡，并刻画量子颤振现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂质非平衡动力学是超冷原子物理的前沿问题，有助于揭示极化子与集体激发的微观机制。相比已广泛研究的排斥相互作用体系，吸引相互作用因不同态之间耦合复杂而缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 研究一维理想费米气体中含带初始动量的吸引型自旋向下杂质，分析自旋间两体关联与杂质动量的时间演化；借助Bethe ansatz精确解，将关联矩阵元简化为有限求和，从而高效计算本征态占据数与长时动力学演化。

Result: 弱吸引区在总动量小于或等于费米动量时出现束缚态特征，较大总动量时呈现混合振荡行为；强吸引区表现为局域束缚态主导的动力学，具有局域化关联峰与散射诱导的Friedel-like振荡。刻画了杂质动量周期性振荡的量子颤振，并通过四种独立Bethe ansatz方法获得一致的杂质临界化学势。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了吸引相互作用下的淬火动力学与量子颤振，深化了对非平衡量子多体性质的理解，并可为相关超冷原子杂质实验提供理论支持。

Abstract: Impurity nonequilibrium dynamics in quantum many-body systems is a frontier subject in ultracold-atom physics, which helps uncover microscopic mechanisms of polaron and collective excitation phenomena. Although repulsive interacting systems have been extensively investigated, attractive-interaction dynamics lacks systematic studies owing to intricate couplings among different states. We study a one-dimensional ideal Fermi gas containing an attractive spin-down impurity with initial momentum and analyze time evolution of interspin two-body correlations and impurity momentum. Using exact Bethe ansatz solutions, we simplify correlation matrix elements to finite sums for efficient calculations of eigenstate occupations and long-time dynamical evolution. In the weakly attractive regime, bound-state features appear when the total momentum is below or equal to the Fermi momentum, while mixed oscillatory behaviors arise at larger total momentum. The strongly attractive regime shows locally bound-state dominated dynamics with localized correlation peaks and scattering-induced Friedel-like oscillations. We characterize quantum flutter, the periodic oscillation of impurity momentum, and obtain consistent critical chemical potential values of the impurity via four independent Bethe ansatz approaches. This work clarifies quench dynamics and quantum flutter under attractive interactions, improves the understanding of nonequilibrium quantum many-body properties, and provides theoretical support for relevant ultracold-atom impurity experiments.

</details>


### [109] [Multi-scale spectral statistics of intermediate quantum chaos in ultracold erbium collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09775)
*Ivan R. R. Gonzalez,Barnali Chakrabarti,Antonio M. S. Macedo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 对超冷166Er和168Er原子共振谱进行多尺度统计分析，发现其谱关联一致地更符合半泊松而非Wigner-Dyson统计，揭示中间量子混沌特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有混合相空间的系统所表现的中间量子混沌需要跨多个尺度探测谱关联，而仅靠最近邻尺度统计不足以刻画其非Wigner-Dyson关联的本质。

Method: 对166Er和168Er超冷原子的共振谱采用多尺度统计诊断，包括高阶间距比、间距增量，以及抽取水平序列的累积功率谱密度分析。

Result: 谱虽表现出明显能级排斥，但一致更符合半泊松而非标准Wigner-Dyson系综，且该中间特征在高阶关联中持续存在；累积功率谱显示中间1/f^alpha区制，满分辨率下alpha约1.5，中等抽取尺度趋向可积随机游走极限alpha=2，表明谱刚性有尺度依赖的损失；间距增量分布介于Laplace与Gaussian极限之间。

Conclusion: 这些互补诊断表明半泊松特征反映了超越最近邻尺度的持续非Wigner-Dyson关联，为中间量子混沌提供了一致图像，并可能关联到碰撞动力学中的分层俘获。

Abstract: Characterizing intermediate quantum chaos in systems with mixed phase spaces requires probing spectral correlations across multiple scales. Here, we present a multi-scale statistical analysis of the resonance spectra of ultracold 166Er and 168Er atoms using higher-order spacing ratios, spacing increments, and the accumulated power spectral density of decimated level sequences. Our analysis reveals that while the spectra exhibit clear level repulsion, they are consistently better described by semi-Poisson than by standard Wigner-Dyson ensembles, with this intermediate character persisting across higher-order correlations. The accumulated power spectrum reveals an intermediate 1/f^alpha regime, with alpha approximately 1.5 at full spectral resolution and a tendency toward the integrable random-walk limit alpha = 2 at intermediate decimation scales, indicating a scale-dependent loss of spectral rigidity. Furthermore, the spacing-increment distributions independently reveal statistics intermediate between Laplace and Gaussian limits. Together, these complementary diagnostics show that the semi-Poisson character reflects persistent non-Wigner-Dyson correlations beyond the nearest-neighbor scale, providing a consistent picture of intermediate quantum chaos and a possible connection to hierarchical trapping in the underlying collision dynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Interaction-enhanced photon blockade and Bell-state protection in microwave-shielded polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09959)
*Yun Chen,Geng Zhao,Huanhuan Wei,Jingjun You,Haoran Jia,Jing Tang,Su Yi,Yuangang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究微波屏蔽极性分子与光学腔耦合的分子腔QED平台，证明屏蔽诱导的各向异性相互作用可统一实现光子阻塞与Bell态保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波屏蔽已成为调控超冷极性分子相互作用的强有力工具，但其在腔量子电动力学（cavity QED）中的潜力尚待探索，尤其是缺乏同时实现少光子非线性与量子态保护的统一机制。

Method: 构建两个微波屏蔽极性分子与单一光学腔模耦合的分子腔QED模型；分析屏蔽诱导的各向异性相互作用对少激发谱非谐性的影响，考察其对多光子跃迁的抑制；并研究相对分子构型（位置涨落）对光子统计的敏感性，以及该相互作用对Bell态耗散的抑制。

Result: 各向异性相互作用通过增强少激发谱的非谐性抑制多光子跃迁，使单光子纯度提升超过三个数量级，并伴随负纵向自旋关联的出现；光子统计对分子相对构型高度敏感，位置变化达相对零点涨落量级即显著改变阻塞性能；同一相互作用通过色散解耦分子激发与损耗腔模，抑制腔介导耗散，减缓初始Bell态的保真度衰减。

Conclusion: 微波屏蔽相互作用可作为统一资源，在分子腔QED系统中同时实现少光子非线性调控与量子态保护。

Abstract: Microwave shielding has recently emerged as a powerful tool for engineering interactions in ultracold polar molecules, yet its potential for controlling cavity quantum electrodynamics remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate a molecular cavity quantum electrodynamics platform in which two microwave-shielded polar molecules are coupled to a single optical cavity mode and demonstrate that shielding-induced interactions provide a unified mechanism for both photon blockade and Bell-state protection. The anisotropic interaction reshapes the few-excitation spectrum by enhancing its anharmonicity, thereby suppressing multiphoton transitions and improving the single-photon purity by more than three orders of magnitude. The enhanced blockade is accompanied by the emergence of negative longitudinal spin correlations, revealing the interaction-induced suppression of simultaneous molecular excitations. We further show that the photon statistics are highly sensitive to the relative molecular configuration, with positional variations on the scale of the relative zero-point fluctuation substantially modifying the blockade performance. Beyond few-photon nonlinear optics, the same interaction protects an initially prepared molecular Bell state by dispersively decoupling molecular excitations from the lossy cavity mode, thereby suppressing cavity-mediated dissipation and slowing the fidelity decay. Our results establish microwave-shielded interactions as a unified interaction resource for engineering few-photon nonlinearities and protecting quantum states in molecular cavity-QED systems.

</details>
